## 0. How Codex / Any AI Agent MUST Use This File

**Read every coding session, before editing:** `PRD.MD` → `AGENTS.md` → `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` → **`todo.md`** → relevant accepted ADRs → actual Git state. If repo facts contradict this tracker, **investigate and correct the tracker with evidence**, never fake repository state.

1. Locate an issue/task by its stable ID (`M0-01` … `M6-08`, or `ISS-0001` …). Do not introduce duplicate work items with new IDs.
2. Check task dependencies and any required **human-accepted ADR**; do not implement security-critical semantics solely from draft proposals.
3. Claim **one bounded task**: change `NOT_STARTED` → `IN_PROGRESS`, fill `Owner`, `Last updated` and the active session log. Multiple agents may own separate tasks but must coordinate files and interfaces.
4. Record code paths, PRD/AC IDs, expected failure cases, tests to run, and security boundary before making changes; put detailed design in ADR/PR rather than bloating this tracker.
5. If blocked, use `BLOCKED`, record the **specific unblock condition** in Blocker Register, and stop unauthorized activity; never convert an unapproved action into a background assumption.
6. After code changes, run appropriate actual checks, preserve negative/security test output, and record command results with commit/PR/file paths in the **Evidence Log**. Do not mark a task DONE when checks have not run or an approval is missing.
7. Use `IN_REVIEW` for completed work awaiting validation/approval. Mark `DONE` only when the architecture task's concrete **Done when** statement is supported by accessible evidence and required review is complete.
8. At the end of **every session**, update the task row(s), milestone summary, Current Focus, Open Issues/Blockers and Session Journal with exact facts and next task. Never claim unseen results.
9. Preserve historical log entries and immutable evidence. If work regresses, reopen the task with an explicit explanation; do **not** erase past DONE/evidence events.
10. Do not let this document become a security authorization token. Task status `DONE` or a written approval note in Markdown **does not** authorize running tests, approving contracts, sending data to an LLM, or changing production policy.

**Status vocabulary — synchronized with `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` §7:**

| Status | Meaning | Entry / exit rule |
|---|---|---|
| `NOT_STARTED` | Planned, no verified task execution yet | Default on creation; can be selected when prerequisites are satisfied |
| `IN_PROGRESS` | Someone is actively working on it | Must have owner, scope and journal entry |
| `BLOCKED` | Stopped by a concrete unmet prerequisite or decision | Blocker ID, unblock condition and next responsible person required |
| `IN_REVIEW` | Work exists, review/tests/evidence gate pending | Include artifact paths, tests, reviewer, unresolved checks |
| `DONE` | Exact work-item DoD and required reviews genuinely met | Verified evidence log + dates + PRD/AC trace required |
| `DEFERRED` | Intentionally postponed by owner | Record rationale, approver and revisit trigger; not counted as done |

**Transitions:** `NOT_STARTED → IN_PROGRESS → IN_REVIEW → DONE`; `IN_PROGRESS/IN_REVIEW → BLOCKED`; `BLOCKED → IN_PROGRESS` after documented resolution; `DONE → IN_PROGRESS` only with explicit reopening event; `NOT_STARTED → DEFERRED` when authorized. Never jump directly from `NOT_STARTED` to `DONE` without logging executed work and verification.

## 4. Approval and Architecture Decision Register

**This table tracks the decision workflow; it does not constitute authenticated approval.** An ADR becomes authoritative only after an actual owner/security reviewer accepts its exact contents via the established review process.

| ADR ID | Related questions | Decision area | Status | Reviewer / proof | Blocks |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ADR-0001` | OQ-02 | Canonical contract/authorization DSL, rule syntax, DENY semantics | `ACCEPTED — specified design choices` | Direct developer decision during M1-01; exact EV-0003 ADR/example hashes verified, EV-0013; M1-02 JSON Schema/stdlib and conservative-overlap clarification, EV-0014 | G-01 DSL choices resolved; other approval/runner/transport/tooling gates unchanged |

- **Bounded implementation sequence after gates:** (1) Resolve actual prerequisites and review physical mapping/test oracles, runtime packages and disposable setup; retain source baseline and environment/version evidence. (2) Implement only lineage needed for immutable contract/version, pinned scenario/fixture/run/result/artifact references and scoped project ancestors; define genuine migration revision/down_revision, per-table ownership and explicit grants/policies. (3) Validate fresh empty upgrade, insert reviewed synthetic old-version baseline then upgrade compatible revision, verify exact bytes/hashes/IDs/joins and no implicit contract reapproval. (4) Exercise negative/role/concurrency/failure/rollback cases below on disposable PostgreSQL using separate migrator and non-owner app roles, then actual restoration/compatibility. Autogenerate output or SQLite/mock tests alone cannot prove PostgreSQL guarantees.
- **Storage invariants to review:** Tenant child rows use non-null scoped composite FKs through org/project and same-project contract/scenario/run/evidence ancestry, with matching parent unique keys; guessed IDs are not grants. Preserve accepted canonical policy bytes/digest/schema/canonicalization/version independently of a JSONB query projection; PostgreSQL JSONB serialization must not silently redefine the policy hash. Unique project+logical-contract+version; old contract body/hash/schema, finalized run/fixture/provenance/artifact links cannot be edited/overwritten. Lifecycle/revocation append separate events; no cascade deleting history without reviewed retention. Stored status/approved_by/hash does not authenticate review; REGISTRY_DISABLED until actual protected authority exists. Least-privilege grants/triggers plus RLS defend ordinary roles, never claim DB-admin resistance or independently anchored audit from SQL constraints. Keep checkpoint/signing authority outside this ordinary store under the already selected audit direction.

- **Required invariants:**Messages carry bounded versioned opaque references, no caller-granted scope/approval, raw policy authority, secrets or executable payload. Heartbeats cannot expand scope or budgets; lost lease fences results but does not kill the process. Unknown side effects block blind replay. Monotonic deadlines within a process plus reviewed persisted UTC/generation/restart/skew rules; never assume a prior process's monotonic reading is comparable after reboot. Cancellation request, acknowledgment, actual stop and cleanup are separate facts. FAILED_INFRA/EXPIRED/CANCELED require observed reasons; never invent a stop or turn worker death/timeout/missing evidence into PASS. Preserve already-observed scenario verdicts and failed history; job COMPLETED may contain ERROR/VIOLATION/INCONCLUSIVE and is not security certification.

No unsanitized HTML report embedded in the privileged app; display inert escaped bounded content until a separately reviewed isolated preview policy exists. Denied/expired/removed downloads, API/schema errors, loading/empty/pagination and stale requests have explicit non-success. - **Proposed ingress/replay requirements:**Use provider-documented cryptographic signature verification on exact bounded raw bytes with approved algorithm/protected secret and constant-time comparison before parsing/admission. Reject absent/malformed/wrong/legacy-disallowed signatures and transformed/tampered body. Header/event/delivery ID/timestamp are not presumed covered by body signature; validate actual protocol and independently trusted mapping, approved event parser and current state. A valid MAC alone does not prove freshness, authorization or unique delivery. Durable per-installation/repository receipt binds delivery metadata, content digest, event/action and immutable PR/revision context; detect identifier/payload mismatch and authenticated duplicate content under a changed untrusted identifier. Approved finite freshness/redelivery/retention policy remains open. Atomic uniqueness and durable outbox/admission linkage survive concurrent delivery, timeout/crash/restart. Legitimate redelivery may retrieve the same committed receipt/handle, never launch another attempt; inconsistent replay is rejected. Store failure blocks dispatch and does not acknowledge successful admission. Do not claim exactly-once execution from receipt deduplication.
- **Proposed scope/credential requirements:**Protected current installation/repository/organization/branch/project mapping is administrator-managed, never supplied by PR JSON. Revalidate suspended/revoked installation and current mapping on ingestion/admission/publication. PR title/body/comments/config/URLs are untrusted data, not target scope, contract approval, commands or destination authority. Cross-check event type/action, repository identity, base/head commit and expected PR context; reject unexpected context. Fork-origin code/secrets exposure remains disabled pending selected policy; no privileged checkout/execution path for untrusted PR code. Shared core still requires anchored approved contract, independent target scope/fixture/runner eligibility and budgets; a signed webhook grants none of those. Short-lived installation-scoped outbound credentials use reviewed least-privilege permissions/destinations; no broad token, caller endpoint/redirect, automatic other installation/provider fallback, secret logs or repository-stored key. Offline mocks exercise guards without deploying ingress or minting actual credentials.
- **Proposed advisory/report requirements:**Serialize shared measured normalized results pinned to the actual repository/head commit/run/policy/fixture/evidence. Publish only through authorized current installation/project context; stale commits/runs cannot replace newer result or overwrite history. Verified VIOLATION and ERROR/INCONCLUSIVE/SKIPPED/CANCELED, blocked/expired/no-run/partial coverage and missing evidence remain explicit non-success, never a fabricated green completion. Job COMPLETED is not all PASS; CI advisory mode does not weaken outcome truth. Exact provider conclusion/status mapping remains a reviewed protocol choice; no live API schema asserted. Include sanitized evidence/reproduction links requiring current access, no bearer secret/public artifact URL. Annotations bind real allowed paths/lines/commit, bounded escaped summaries and observed facts; separate AI hypotheses and omit secret/raw private snippets. Publication timeout/rate/error does not fabricate posted success or PASS; durable fake-publisher retry tests preserve the same pinned intent. Default advisory; no branch-protection/admin-gate changes or auto-merge.

- **Concrete new proposal, not accepted design:** Pure non-authoritative simulated scope descriptors and strict origin/path checks with explicit scheme/host/port/IP/method/path declarations. Candidate/mock allowlists cannot establish authenticated contract or scope approval. Literal ASCII normalized hosts/IPs, exact origins, no wildcards or ambient proxy/environment trust; reject userinfo, ambiguous host/port spelling, unsupported schemes, fragments, query/credential injection, controls/backslashes, traversal, unsafe percent/double encodings and undeclared paths. - **DNS/peer proposal:** Inject a fake resolver and fake connector only; never call a real resolver/socket/client. Validate all A/AAAA-like observations against explicit candidate IP scope; reject empty/mixed/forbidden answer sets before connect. Pin a selected declared IP into the fake connection; preserve verified host/TLS identity metadata. Check the observed peer before any simulated HTTP/credential bytes. Revalidate each new connection/retry, reject out-of-scope changed DNS/peer and unsupported pinning/TLS/proxy capabilities rather than silently fall back. This demonstrates ordering in a mock trace, not actual OS/client rebinding resistance or a grant to contact any address.
- **Budget/cancellation proposal:** Explicit finite request/resolution/concurrency/rate/deadline/timeout/response-byte budgets and injected monotonic clock; strict exact integer validation, no unlimited defaults or model-supplied overrides. Count attempts at admission, block before unsafe admission, retain exhausted budgets, check cancellation/time before resolution/connect/send and during bounded response observation, release simulated connection/concurrency resources in finally paths. Reserve any future fixture cleanup capability separately; no real cleanup, blanket scope extension or execution-grant renewal. Limits/cancellation/failed DNS/peer/capability observations yield explicit non-PASS typed errors, never security PASS. Numeric values in tests are mock inputs, not accepted deployment budgets.
- **Positive/negative success matrix, PLANNED:** An explicitly declared synthetic origin/path/IP is admitted through the mock trace only. Disallowed external origin/scheme/port/path and credentials rejected before resolve/connect/send; empty/mixed DNS and a changed out-of-scope answer rejected before connect; mismatched peer before send; every redirect emits zero follow-up requests and never forwards credentials. IPv4/IPv6, lookalike/trailing-dot/numeric hosts, traversal/encoded separators, ambiguous URL/control strings and unknown declarations tested. ## 9. Change Control and Multi-Agent Coordination

- Prefer **one source of status truth**: this root `todo.md`. Do not keep a separate hand-edited `docs/ROADMAP.md` status ledger; that file, if created, should summarize or link to the live tracker.
- `PRD.MD` defines requirements; `AGENTS.md` agent conduct; `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` WBS and DoD; ADRs approved choices; **this file records actual work evidence and current status**.
- Agent must inspect repo state and this ledger before picking a task; if work-in-progress owner/task already exists, coordinate ownership rather than edit the same files.
- In parallel work, claim explicit paths/modules and agree schema changes first. Use independent branches/PRs for conflicting areas. Merge the task tracker carefully and recompute milestone counts after resolving conflicts.
- All status and evidence changes belong in version control. Never silently delete a blocked/disputed issue, approval history or prior test failure.
- Changing WBS scope/priority/dependencies requires an update to `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`/`PRD.MD` through the documented change process first; this ledger only mirrors the authoritative approved plan.
- New unexpected bugs go into `ISS-####`; newly approved feature work must first appear in architecture WBS (or an explicitly approved extension section), then the tracker.
- Do not edit tracked acceptance wording to make unfinished work look finished.
- Before ending a session, publish a handoff: task, status, diff, tests, security implications, blockers and next task.

### Example status update (illustration only — not completed work)

```markdown
| `M1-07` | Scope guard and request-budget policy | M0-04/M1-05 | `IN_REVIEW` | @security-agent | Out-of-scope redirect blocked before outbound request | `EV-0021`; tests/security/test_scope.py; PR #42 |
```

This example is **hypothetical**, not a record of executed work.
